# Catalog sources

Generated by `python3 scripts/catalog/build.py` from `catalog-data/sources/*.json`. Do not edit by hand.

Every product records the official page its details were read from. Prices are TrustKart **virtual** reference prices, not live retail prices:

- **msrp**: manufacturer's published list price for the configuration described.
- **starting-at**: manufacturer's published starting price; the configurations spec lists the options.
- **estimate**: no public manufacturer price (quote-only enterprise hardware, or price not published). The number is a TrustKart simulation value and must not be read as a quote.

Built 2026-09-29. 2088 products from 42 source files.

## apple-acc-audio (48)

Apple-store audio & small accessories batch (apple-acc-audio): Beats headphones/earbuds/speaker, Beats cables, cushion kits and Beats iPhone cases; AirPods ear tips/cushions and third-party AirPods cases/charms/lanyards sold on apple.com; AirTag FineWoven key ring and third-party AirTag holders; Siri Remote and Apple TV HDMI cable. All products, prices (Apple Store US, msrp) and colours read from apple.com/shop product pages and their productVariations data on 2026-09-29. Images are Apple Store per-part product images (store.storeimages.cdn-apple.com, png-alpha 1400px). Beats 360 battery figure from beatsbydre.com. Beats Fit Pro is no longer listed on apple.com/us (Powerbeats Fit is), so it is not included.

## apple-acc-iphone (75)

iPhone cases, screen protectors, MagSafe wallets, straps, grips and carry organizers sold on apple.com/us/shop/iphone/accessories/cases-protection for current iPhones (iPhone 18 Pro, 18 Pro Max, Air, 17, 17e, 16, Duo). Researched 2026-09-29 from apple.com/shop product pages (ld+json offers, variant part maps, Overview and Compatibility panels). Prices are Apple Store US prices. One product per case line per iPhone model; colours in colors/colorImages using the Apple Store per-part product images (store.storeimages.cdn-apple.com). Per coordinator, still-sold cases and protectors for older supported iPhones (17 Pro/Pro Max, 16 Plus/Pro/Pro Max, 16e, 15/15 Plus/15 Pro Max) are included too; iPhone 14 and earlier are not. No OtterBox, Speck, Pela or Zagg products were listed on the page at research time. A second Herschel Cloud Sling listing (Blue/Pink, HSKQ2ZM/A) exists with a different compatibility list; only the Pink/Black HSFC2ZM/A listing is recorded. All Beats iPhone cases (17, Air, 17e, 16 incl. Special Edition colours) and the Tech21 Wrist Lanyard are recorded in apple-acc-audio.json and are not repeated here.

Mac Pro, Pro Display XDR and Apple Pencil (1st/2nd gen, legacy iPads only) are omitted: Mac Pro and Pro Display XDR are no longer listed on apple.com/mac. ## dell (49)

Dell and Alienware current lineup (dell.com US), researched 2026-09-28: 2026 XPS relaunch, Dell / Dell Plus, Dell Pro (Premium, 7 Series, Plus), Dell Pro Max and Dell Pro Precision mobile and tower workstations, Dell Pro Max with GB10/GB300, Alienware Area-51/Aurora, Alienware/UltraSharp/Dell Pro monitors, Dell Pro Thunderbolt 5 docks and 17G PowerEdge rack, tower and XE AI servers. PC prices are the dell.com price of the pre-configured model shown on each product page (specs describe that configuration); 'build your own' starting prices are listed in variants where Dell shows one. Monitor and dock prices come from dell.com listing pages when the product page did not render a price. PowerEdge R/T prices are dell.com 'Starting at' configure-to-order prices; XE AI servers and the Pro Max GB300 are quote-only, so their prices are estimates. Images are dell.com product gallery images from i.dell.com (rights not verified). Dell gallery images are cropped edge-to-edge; build.py detects the white studio background from the corners and re-pads them. The PowerEdge R670 URL returned the same asset as the R470 and was dropped.

Flash drives & memory cards (SanDisk, Samsung, ProGrade Digital, Lexar and others). Researched 2026-09-28 from official manufacturer stores/pages only: shop.sandisk.com (list prices from SanDisk store price API, not sale prices), samsung.com/us (msrpPrice), shop.progradedigital.com (Shopify single-card prices), americas.lexar.com (specs; Lexar US site shows no prices, so Lexar prices are estimates), pny.com (specs/images; prices render client-side, so PNY prices are estimates), apricorn.com (starting prices) and angelbird.com (JSON-LD USD offers). Kingston could not be researched: kingston.com / shop.kingston.com serve a bot-verification challenge, which was not bypassed. Card readers intentionally excluded.

## hp (35)

HP (incl. HyperX, OMEN) and HPE products researched 2026-09-28 on hp.com US store (HP Store PDPs read in a normal browser) and buy.hpe.com. Prices are HP/HPE Store MSRP or configurable starting-at (list) prices, not sale prices; several items had instant savings on the day. HP business SKUs (EliteBook/ZBook/Z) carry HP's high list MSRPs incl. 3-yr warranty + Wolf Pro Security. HP rebranded gaming to 'HyperX OMEN' in 2026. Images are HP Store gallery images from hp.widen.net. Research cut short by usage limits; HPE Aruba networking, more ProLiant models and LaserJet Enterprise not covered.

## lenovo (48)

Lenovo consumer and commercial client lineup from lenovo.com (US), researched 2026-09-28: ThinkPad X1/X9/T/E/X13 and P-series mobile workstations, ThinkBook, Yoga, Lenovo Slim/Pro, IdeaPad, Legion laptops and towers, Legion Go S, Lenovo tablets, and Legion/Yoga/ThinkVision monitors. Existing Lenovo items (demo ThinkPad X1 Carbon Gen 13, P16 Gen 2, ThinkStation PX, ThinkCentre, ThinkSystem servers) are not duplicated. Specs come from the lenovo.com product-page tech-spec tables (cross-linked to psref.lenovo.com). Prices are the lenovo.com 'starting at' price of each series page on the retrieval date (Lenovo's current sale price; Lenovo's higher pre-discount web price is recorded in priceNote); spec values describe the entry option listed on that page. Images: lenovo.com product-gallery PNGs at their native 584 px (the CDN's ?width= parameter only upscales, so it is not used) or, where cleaner/larger, 2000 px PSREF renders from psrefstuff.lenovo.com; PSREF renders carrying Intel/Copilot+ badges were rejected. Temporarily unavailable models (Legion Go Gen 2, Yoga Book 9i, ThinkBook Plus Rollable, LOQ 15AHP11, Chromebook Plus 14 MediaTek) and the Legion 7i Gen 10 / Legion R45w-30 (no clean official image) were left out.

## net-enterprise (52)

Enterprise and SMB networking (net-enterprise): Cisco/Meraki, Dell PowerSwitch, Netgate, Palo Alto Networks, SonicWall, RUCKUS, and others. Researched 2026-09-28 from official manufacturer sites and datasheets. Most enterprise vendors publish no US list price; those records use priceBasis 'estimate' and say so in notes. Netgate prices are Netgate store prices.

## net-home-smb (55)

Home and small-office networking from TP-Link, NETGEAR, eero, Linksys, Zyxel, D-Link, Synology, QNAP, TRENDnet and Firewalla: Wi-Fi 7 routers, mesh systems, 2.5G/10G unmanaged and smart switches, PoE switches, SMB gateways and firewalls, powerline/MoCA and USB/PCIe adapters. Researched 2026-09-28 from official manufacturer sites and stores only. Where a maker sells only through retailers in the US (e.g. TP-Link, Synology), priceBasis is 'estimate' and the notes say so. Linksys was not included: linksys.com no longer runs a US store and only publishes ~340px product images. Several NETGEAR Nighthawk/Orbi and eero consumer models were left out because the makers only publish product photos too small for the storefront image pipeline.

## net-ubiquiti-mikrotik (66)

Ubiquiti (UniFi switches, access points, gateways, consoles, power, racks, cabling and UISP), MikroTik and GL.iNet networking gear complementing networking.json. Researched 2026-09-28 from store.ui.com (product JSON on the official US store), mikrotik.com product pages ('Suggested price') and the official GL.iNet store (www.gl-inet.com, which store.gl-inet.com redirects to). Ubiquiti prices are UI Store US base list prices; the store currently itemizes a separate memory surcharge at checkout for many products, recorded in priceNote. OnePlus was in the brief but is intentionally absent: oneplus.com/us posts a 'Notice of Business Adjustment' (OnePlus has concluded product rollouts in North America) and every US store listing (OnePlus 15, 15R, Buds 4, Buds Pro 3, Watch 3, Pad accessories) is out of stock or 'stop sale'. ## razer-framework-lg (48)

Razer, Framework and LG (LG Electronics) current products researched 2026-09-28 from official US sites: razer.com (embedded store product data: prices, specs, stock), frame.work (spec tabs and configurator/marketplace prices; many configs are pre-order), lg.com/us (product pages: list MSRP, spec tables, gallery images) and LG USA press release for 2026 OLED MSRPs. LG prices are LG.com list (MSRP) prices; current LG.com sale prices are noted in priceNote. Framework DIY Edition prices exclude memory/storage/OS. Razer Blade 14 and Framework Laptop 13 (AI 300)/Desktop were listed but out of stock on the research date. Razer product images are the official 500x500 store PNGs (largest clean studio asset found). Razer images omitted: the only clean studio assets on razer.com are 500x500 PNGs (product occupies <480 px after trimming, below the pipeline minimum); gallery images are dark lifestyle shots. LG 39GX950B/45GX950B use badge-free angled gallery shots (gallery-13 / gallery_16). Razer Cobra Pro uses the transparent hero render from its razer.com landing page. Razer publishes no larger clean studio images (re-checked 2026-09-28: store gallery 1500x1000 images and every press.razer.com newsroom asset are dark/lifestyle scenes), so 22 Razer products use the official 500x500 store PNGs with allowLowRes. Still omitted: Blade 14 (its only studio PNG trims to 305 px, below the 320 px low-res floor) and BlackShark V3 Pro (standard black/white PNGs are 300x300 natively; only special-edition artwork PNGs are larger). BlackShark V3 uses the same-design BlackShark V3 for Xbox White image (PRODUCT_LINE).
